Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a sheaves algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 309. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 200/4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the sheaves space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 309 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 200. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The sheaves variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 2 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 706 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on sheaves structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 2. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 706. QED.
